    /* Header: instead of letting everything wrap wherever it happens to fit,

       give each group its own full-width row, in a fixed, predictable order.

       That's what actually fixes the mobile layout — the old version could

       wrap into four or five uneven rows depending on button widths. */

<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@supabase/supabase-js@2/dist/umd/supabase.js"></script>

<script>

/* ============================================================

   Pastoral roster engine

   Pure logic: no DOM. Shared by the app and the node tests.

   ============================================================ */


const PASTORS = [

  { id: 'LG', last: 'Leigeber', name: 'Fr. Leigeber', color: '#7B2D5E' },

  { id: 'SU', last: 'Suelzle',  name: 'Fr. Suelzle',  color: '#1F5F52' },

  { id: 'LK', last: 'Leckband', name: 'Fr. Leckband', color: '#A63A2A' },

  { id: 'LH', last: 'Lehman',   name: 'Fr. Lehman',   color: '#2C4A7C' },

];

const PIDS = PASTORS.map(p => p.id);

const PMAP = Object.fromEntries(PASTORS.map(p => [p.id, p]));


/* Allowed [celebrant, partner] pairings, verbatim from the parish rules. */

const PAIRS = [

  ['LG', 'SU'],

  ['LG', 'LH'],

  ['LG', 'LK'],

  ['SU', 'LH'],

  ['SU', 'LK'],

];

const CELEBRANT_ELIGIBLE = ['LG', 'SU'];      // except Evening Prayer

const partnersFor = cel => PAIRS.filter(p => p[0] === cel).map(p => p[1]);

const pairAllowed = (cel, other) =>

  !other || cel === other || PAIRS.some(p => p[0] === cel && p[1] === other);


const ROLES = ['celebrant', 'deacon', 'subdeacon', 'preacher'];

const ROLE_LABEL = {

  celebrant: 'Celebrant/Liturgist',

  deacon: 'Deacon',

  subdeacon: 'Subdeacon',

  preacher: 'Preacher',

};

const ROLE_SHORT = { celebrant: 'C', deacon: 'D', subdeacon: 'Sub.D.', preacher: 'P' };


/* Weekly pattern of services. dow: 0=Sun … 6=Sat */

const WEEKLY_TEMPLATE = [

  { dow: 0, time: '09:30', name: 'Divine Service', roles: ['celebrant', 'deacon', 'preacher'] },

  { dow: 1, time: '08:15', name: 'Matins',         roles: ['celebrant', 'preacher'] },

  { dow: 2, time: '08:15', name: 'Matins',         roles: ['celebrant'] },

  { dow: 3, time: '08:15', name: 'Divine Service', roles: ['celebrant', 'deacon', 'preacher'] },

  { dow: 3, time: '18:30', name: 'Evening Prayer', roles: ['celebrant', 'preacher'] },

  { dow: 4, time: '08:15', name: 'Matins',         roles: ['celebrant'] },

  { dow: 5, time: '08:15', name: 'Matins',         roles: ['celebrant', 'preacher'] },

];


const DEFAULT_SETTINGS = {

  weights: { LG: 40, SU: 40, LK: 10, LH: 10 },

  leckbandInterval: 6,   // minimum weeks between Fr. Leckband's sermons

  maxSermonsPerWeek: 2,

  alternationAnchor: 0,  // 0 → Fr. Leigeber has the first Sunday of the epoch week

  capLeckbandToInterval: true,

  eveningPrayerPrefersJuniors: true,

  noSundayPreaching: ['LH'],

};


/* ---------- date helpers (local time, no UTC drift) ---------- */

const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

const iso = d => `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}`;

const fromISO = s => { const [y, m, d] = s.split('-').map(Number); return new Date(y, m - 1, d); };

const addDays = (d, n) => new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate() + n);

const startOfWeek = d => addDays(d, -d.getDay());              // weeks run Sunday→Saturday

const EPOCH_SUNDAY = new Date(2024, 0, 7);

const weekIndexOf = sunday =>

  Math.round((sunday - EPOCH_SUNDAY) / 604800000);


/* ---------- generator ---------- */


/* The stated weights and Fr. Leckband's six-week interval cannot both hold:

   10% of five sermons a week is one every fortnight. When the interval cap is

   on, we honour the interval, then redistribute the shortfall across the other

   men in proportion to their stated weights. Returns the shares actually used. */

function effectiveTargets(settings) {

  settings = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, ...settings };

  const w = settings.weights;

  const sum = PIDS.reduce((s, p) => s + (w[p] || 0), 0) || 1;

  const stated = Object.fromEntries(PIDS.map(p => [p, (w[p] || 0) / sum]));

  if (!settings.capLeckbandToInterval) return stated;


  const slotsPerWeek = WEEKLY_TEMPLATE.filter(t => t.roles.includes('preacher')).length;

  const cap = 1 / (settings.leckbandInterval * slotsPerWeek);

  if (stated.LK <= cap) return stated;


  const eff = { ...stated, LK: cap };

  const rest = PIDS.filter(p => p !== 'LK');

  const restSum = rest.reduce((s, p) => s + stated[p], 0) || 1;

  const surplus = stated.LK - cap;

  rest.forEach(p => { eff[p] = stated[p] + surplus * (stated[p] / restSum); });

  return eff;

}


function makeState(settings) {

  return {

    target: effectiveTargets(settings),

    sermons: Object.fromEntries(PIDS.map(p => [p, 0])),

    celebrantDuty: Object.fromEntries(PIDS.map(p => [p, 0])),

    deaconDuty: Object.fromEntries(PIDS.map(p => [p, 0])),

    totalSermons: 0,

    lastLeckbandWeek: -Infinity,

    lastSundayDeacon: null,

  };

}


/* Seed counters from services that already exist before the generated range,

   so a partial regeneration continues the rotation instead of restarting it. */

function seedState(state, services, beforeISO, settings) {

  services

    .filter(s => s.date < beforeISO && !s.deleted)

    .sort((a, b) => (a.date + a.time).localeCompare(b.date + b.time))

    .forEach(s => {

      const a = s.assign || {};

      if (a.preacher) {

        state.sermons[a.preacher] = (state.sermons[a.preacher] || 0) + 1;

        state.totalSermons++;

        if (a.preacher === 'LK') {

          state.lastLeckbandWeek = weekIndexOf(startOfWeek(fromISO(s.date)));

        }

      }

      if (a.celebrant) state.celebrantDuty[a.celebrant]++;

      if (a.deacon) {

        state.deaconDuty[a.deacon]++;

        if (fromISO(s.date).getDay() === 0) state.lastSundayDeacon = a.deacon;

      }

    });

  return state;

}


/* Largest-deficit choice: keeps the running distribution pinned to the weights. */

function deficit(state, p) {

  return state.target[p] * (state.totalSermons + 1) - state.sermons[p];

}


function canPreach(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, p, opts, settings) {

  if (opts.sunday && settings.noSundayPreaching.includes(p)) return false;

  if ((weekPreach[p] || 0) >= settings.maxSermonsPerWeek) return false;

  if (p === 'LK' && weekIdx - state.lastLeckbandWeek < settings.leckbandInterval) return false;

  return true;

}


function bestPreacher(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, cands, opts, settings) {

  const ok = cands.filter(p => canPreach(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, p, opts, settings));

  const pool = ok.length ? ok

    : cands.filter(p => !(opts.sunday && settings.noSundayPreaching.includes(p)));

  if (!pool.length) return cands[0] || null;

  return pool.slice().sort((a, b) =>

    (deficit(state, b) - deficit(state, a)) ||

    (state.sermons[a] - state.sermons[b]) ||

    (PIDS.indexOf(a) - PIDS.indexOf(b))

  )[0];

}


function recordSermon(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, p) {

  state.sermons[p]++;

  state.totalSermons++;

  weekPreach[p] = (weekPreach[p] || 0) + 1;

  if (p === 'LK') state.lastLeckbandWeek = weekIdx;

}


/* Pick deacon + preacher together for a three-role Divine Service. */

function pickDeaconAndPreacher(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, cel, opts, settings) {

  const options = [];

  for (const d of partnersFor(cel)) {

    for (const pr of [cel, d]) {

      if (opts.sunday && settings.noSundayPreaching.includes(pr)) continue;

      if (!canPreach(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, pr, opts, settings)) continue;

      let score = deficit(state, pr);

      score -= state.deaconDuty[d] * 0.012;                     // spread deacon turns

      if (opts.sunday && d === state.lastSundayDeacon) score -= 0.06;

      options.push({ d, pr, score });

    }

  }

  if (!options.length) {

    const d = partnersFor(cel)

      .slice()

      .sort((a, b) => state.deaconDuty[a] - state.deaconDuty[b])[0];

    return { deacon: d, preacher: cel };

  }

  options.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);

  return { deacon: options[0].d, preacher: options[0].pr };

}


/**

 * Build assignments for one liturgical week.

 * Returns { [dow-time]: {celebrant, deacon, preacher} }

 */

function assignWeek(state, sunday, settings) {

  const weekIdx = weekIndexOf(sunday);

  const weekPreach = {};

  const out = {};


  /* 1. Sunday — Leigeber and Suelzle alternate as celebrant. */

  const sunCel = (weekIdx + settings.alternationAnchor) % 2 === 0 ? 'LG' : 'SU';

  const sun = pickDeaconAndPreacher(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, sunCel, { sunday: true }, settings);

  out['0-09:30'] = { celebrant: sunCel, deacon: sun.deacon, preacher: sun.preacher };

  recordSermon(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, sun.preacher);

  state.celebrantDuty[sunCel]++;

  state.deaconDuty[sun.deacon]++;

  state.lastSundayDeacon = sun.deacon;


  /* 2. Sunday's preacher takes Tuesday and Thursday Matins. */

  const weekdayCel = CELEBRANT_ELIGIBLE.includes(sun.preacher) ? sun.preacher : sunCel;

  out['2-08:15'] = { celebrant: weekdayCel };

  out['4-08:15'] = { celebrant: weekdayCel };

  state.celebrantDuty[weekdayCel] += 2;


  /* 3. Wednesday Divine Service — the other senior man celebrates. */

  const wedCel = sunCel === 'LG' ? 'SU' : 'LG';

  const wed = pickDeaconAndPreacher(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, wedCel, {}, settings);

  out['3-08:15'] = { celebrant: wedCel, deacon: wed.deacon, preacher: wed.preacher };

  recordSermon(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, wed.preacher);

  state.celebrantDuty[wedCel]++;

  state.deaconDuty[wed.deacon]++;


  /* 4. Wednesday Evening Prayer — one man liturgises and preaches.

        This is the only service the junior men may liturgise, so by default

        it is offered to them first. */

  let epPool = PIDS;

  if (settings.eveningPrayerPrefersJuniors) {

    const juniors = ['LK', 'LH'].filter(p =>

      canPreach(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, p, {}, settings));

    if (juniors.length) epPool = juniors;

  }

  const ep = bestPreacher(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, epPool, {}, settings);

  out['3-18:30'] = { celebrant: ep, preacher: ep };

  recordSermon(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, ep);

  state.celebrantDuty[ep]++;


  /* 5. Monday and Friday Matins. */

  for (const dow of [1, 5]) {

    const pr = bestPreacher(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, PIDS, {}, settings);

    let cel;

    if (CELEBRANT_ELIGIBLE.includes(pr)) {

      cel = pr;

    } else {

      cel = CELEBRANT_ELIGIBLE

        .filter(c => pairAllowed(c, pr))

        .sort((a, b) => state.celebrantDuty[a] - state.celebrantDuty[b])[0] || 'LG';

    }

    out[`${dow}-08:15`] = { celebrant: cel, preacher: pr };

    recordSermon(state, weekPreach, weekIdx, pr);

    state.celebrantDuty[cel]++;

  }


  return out;

}


/**

 * Generate services across a date range.

 * Existing services are preserved when locked, special, or outside the range.

 */

function generateRange(startISO, endISO, settings, existing = []) {

  settings = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, ...settings };

  const keep = existing.filter(s =>

    s.date < startISO || s.date > endISO || s.locked || s.special || s.deleted

  );

  const keptKey = new Set(keep.map(s => `${s.date}|${s.time}|${s.name}`));


  const state = seedState(makeState(settings), existing, startISO, settings);

  const built = [];

  const end = fromISO(endISO);


  for (let sunday = startOfWeek(fromISO(startISO)); sunday <= end; sunday = addDays(sunday, 7)) {

    const week = assignWeek(state, sunday, settings);

    for (const tpl of WEEKLY_TEMPLATE) {

      const day = addDays(sunday, tpl.dow);

      const dISO = iso(day);

      if (dISO < startISO || dISO > endISO) continue;

      const k = `${dISO}|${tpl.time}|${tpl.name}`;

      if (keptKey.has(k)) continue;

      const assign = week[`${tpl.dow}-${tpl.time}`] || {};

      built.push({

        id: `${dISO}-${tpl.time.replace(':', '')}-${tpl.name.replace(/\s+/g, '')}`,

        date: dISO,

        time: tpl.time,

        name: tpl.name,

        roles: tpl.roles.slice(),

        assign: Object.fromEntries(tpl.roles.map(r => [r, assign[r] || null])),

        locked: false,

        special: false,

      });

    }

  }

  return [...keep, ...built].sort((a, b) =>

    (a.date + a.time).localeCompare(b.date + b.time));

}


/* ---------- rule checker: validates the schedule after hand edits ---------- */


function checkRules(services, settings) {

  settings = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, ...settings };

  services = services.filter(s => !s.deleted);

  const issues = [];

  const flag = (date, msg) => issues.push({ date, msg });

  const nm = id => (PMAP[id] ? PMAP[id].last : id);


  for (const s of services) {

    const a = s.assign || {};

    const men = [...new Set(s.roles.map(r => a[r]).filter(Boolean))];

    const highFeast = s.roles.includes('subdeacon'); // the rare 3-minister exception


    for (const r of s.roles) {

      if (!a[r]) flag(s.date, `${s.name}: ${ROLE_LABEL[r]} is unassigned`);

    }

    const maxMen = highFeast ? 3 : 2;

    if (men.length > maxMen) {

      flag(s.date, `${s.name}: ${men.length} pastors assigned — never more than ${maxMen === 3 ? 'three' : 'two'}`);

    }

    if (s.name === 'Evening Prayer') {

      if (a.celebrant && a.preacher && a.celebrant !== a.preacher) {

        flag(s.date, 'Evening Prayer: liturgist and preacher must be the same man');

      }

    } else if (a.celebrant && !CELEBRANT_ELIGIBLE.includes(a.celebrant)) {

      flag(s.date, `${s.name}: ${nm(a.celebrant)} does not serve as Celebrant/Liturgist`);

    }

    if (!highFeast && a.celebrant && a.deacon && !pairAllowed(a.celebrant, a.deacon)) {

      flag(s.date, `${s.name}: ${nm(a.celebrant)} + ${nm(a.deacon)} is not an approved pairing`);

    }

    if (s.roles.includes('deacon') && a.preacher &&

        a.preacher !== a.celebrant && a.preacher !== a.deacon && a.preacher !== a.subdeacon) {

      flag(s.date, `${s.name}: the preacher must also be one of the assigned ministers`);

    }

    if (fromISO(s.date).getDay() === 0 && a.preacher &&

        settings.noSundayPreaching.includes(a.preacher)) {

      flag(s.date, `Sunday: ${nm(a.preacher)} does not preach on Sundays`);

    }

  }


  /* week-scoped rules */

  const weeks = {};

  for (const s of services) {

    const k = iso(startOfWeek(fromISO(s.date)));

    (weeks[k] ||= []).push(s);

  }

  const lkWeeks = [];

  Object.keys(weeks).sort().forEach(k => {

    const list = weeks[k];

    const counts = {};

    list.forEach(s => {

      const p = s.assign && s.assign.preacher;

      if (p) counts[p] = (counts[p] || 0) + 1;

    });

    for (const [p, n] of Object.entries(counts)) {

      if (n > settings.maxSermonsPerWeek) {

        flag(k, `Week of ${k}: ${nm(p)} preaches ${n} times (target is ${settings.maxSermonsPerWeek})`);

      }

    }

    if (counts.LK) lkWeeks.push(weekIndexOf(fromISO(k)));


    const sun = list.find(s => fromISO(s.date).getDay() === 0 && s.roles.includes('deacon'));

    const sunPreacher = sun && sun.assign && sun.assign.preacher;

    if (sunPreacher && CELEBRANT_ELIGIBLE.includes(sunPreacher)) {

      for (const dow of [2, 4]) {

        const m = list.find(s => fromISO(s.date).getDay() === dow && s.name === 'Matins');

        if (m && m.assign.celebrant && m.assign.celebrant !== sunPreacher) {

          flag(m.date, `${['Sun','Mon','Tue','Wed','Thu','Fri','Sat'][dow]} Matins: should be ${nm(sunPreacher)}, who preached Sunday`);

        }

      }

    }

  });


  for (let i = 1; i < lkWeeks.length; i++) {

    const gap = lkWeeks[i] - lkWeeks[i - 1];

    if (gap < settings.leckbandInterval) {

      flag(null, `Fr. Leckband preaches only ${gap} week(s) apart (target is every ${settings.leckbandInterval})`);

    }

  }


  /* Sunday alternation */

  const sundays = services

    .filter(s => fromISO(s.date).getDay() === 0 && s.roles.includes('deacon'))

    .sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));

  for (let i = 1; i < sundays.length; i++) {

    const prev = sundays[i - 1].assign.celebrant, cur = sundays[i].assign.celebrant;

    const consecutive =

      weekIndexOf(startOfWeek(fromISO(sundays[i].date))) -

      weekIndexOf(startOfWeek(fromISO(sundays[i - 1].date))) === 1;

    if (consecutive && prev && cur && prev === cur) {

      flag(sundays[i].date, `Sunday: ${nm(cur)} celebrates two Sundays running — they should alternate`);

    }

  }

  return issues;

}


/* ---------- tallies ---------- */

function tally(services) {

  services = services.filter(s => !s.deleted);

  const t = Object.fromEntries(PIDS.map(p => [p, { preacher: 0, celebrant: 0, deacon: 0, total: 0 }]));

  let sermons = 0;

  for (const s of services) {

    for (const r of s.roles) {

      const p = s.assign && s.assign[r];

      if (!p || !t[p]) continue;

      t[p][r]++;

      t[p].total++;

      if (r === 'preacher') sermons++;

    }

  }

  return { byPastor: t, sermons };

}


if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {

  module.exports = {

    PASTORS, PIDS, PMAP, PAIRS, ROLES, ROLE_LABEL, ROLE_SHORT,

    CELEBRANT_ELIGIBLE, WEEKLY_TEMPLATE, DEFAULT_SETTINGS,

    iso, fromISO, addDays, startOfWeek, weekIndexOf, partnersFor, pairAllowed,

    generateRange, checkRules, tally, effectiveTargets,

  };

}


</script>

const LIT_COLOR = {

  green:  '#3E6B4A',

  white:  '#C9C2AC',

  violet: '#5B3C8C',

  red:    '#9E2B2B',

  rose:   '#C17A93',

  black:  '#2B2B2B',

};

const LIT_LABEL = { green:'Green', white:'White', violet:'Violet', red:'Red',

                    rose:'Rose', black:'Black' };


/* ---------- importing a new year's calendar from an .xlsx file ----------

   Ports the same reading logic used to build the calendar in the first place:

   walk the "Calendar" sheet, column A is a month name on the first day of each

   month, B is the day number, C the weekday abbreviation, D carries the

   liturgical color as a cell fill (no text), E the feast/commemoration name,

   F the readings, G a secondary commemoration. Everything is parsed straight

   from the workbook's XML, so no server round trip is needed. */

const MONTH_NUM = Object.fromEntries(MONTHS.map((m, i) => [m, i + 1]));

const XLSX_FILL_TO_NAME = {

  'FF274E13': 'green', 'FFEFEFEF': 'white', 'FF351C75': 'violet',

  'FF980000': 'red',   'FFA64D79': 'rose',  'FF000000': 'black',

};


function colLetterToIndex(ref) {

  const m = /^([A-Z]+)/.exec(ref);

  let n = 0;

  for (const ch of m[1]) n = n * 26 + (ch.charCodeAt(0) - 64);

  return n;

}


async function ensureJSZip() {

  if (window.JSZip) return;

  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {

    const s = document.createElement('script');

    s.src = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/jszip/3.10.1/jszip.min.js';

    s.onload = resolve;

    s.onerror = () => reject(new Error(

      "Couldn't load the file reader. Check your connection and try again."));

    document.head.appendChild(s);

  });

}


/* Unzip + read the raw grid of {value, rgb} cells, keyed by 1-based column

   index. This step doesn't need a year, so it only has to run once per file

   even if the person changes the "church year begins in" field afterward. */

async function readWorkbookGrid(arrayBuffer) {

  await ensureJSZip();

  let zip;

  try { zip = await window.JSZip.loadAsync(arrayBuffer); }

  catch (e) { throw new Error("That doesn't look like an Excel (.xlsx) file."); }


  const parser = new DOMParser();

  const need = async path => {

    const f = zip.file(path);

    if (!f) return null;

    return parser.parseFromString(await f.async('string'), 'application/xml');

  };


  const wbDoc = await need('xl/workbook.xml');

  const relsDoc = await need('xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels');

  if (!wbDoc || !relsDoc) throw new Error("This file isn't a standard Excel workbook.");


  const sheets = [...wbDoc.getElementsByTagName('sheet')];

  let target = sheets.find(s => (s.getAttribute('name') || '').trim().toLowerCase() === 'calendar');

  let usedFallback = false;

  if (!target) { target = sheets[0]; usedFallback = true; }

  if (!target) throw new Error('No worksheets found in this file.');


  const rId = target.getAttribute('r:id') ||

    target.getAttributeNS('http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships', 'id');

  const rel = [...relsDoc.getElementsByTagName('Relationship')].find(r => r.getAttribute('Id') === rId);

  if (!rel) throw new Error('Could not locate that worksheet inside the file.');

  let path = rel.getAttribute('Target');

  if (!path.startsWith('xl/')) path = 'xl/' + path.replace(/^\/?/, '');


  const sheetDoc = await need(path);

  if (!sheetDoc) throw new Error('The worksheet data is missing from this file.');


  let sharedStrings = [];

  const ssDoc = await need('xl/sharedStrings.xml');

  if (ssDoc) {

    sharedStrings = [...ssDoc.getElementsByTagName('si')].map(si =>

      [...si.getElementsByTagName('t')].map(t => t.textContent).join(''));

  }


  let styleFill = [];

  const stylesDoc = await need('xl/styles.xml');

  if (stylesDoc) {

    const fillsNode = stylesDoc.getElementsByTagName('fills')[0];

    const fillRgb = (fillsNode ? [...fillsNode.getElementsByTagName('fill')] : []).map(f => {

      const pf = f.getElementsByTagName('patternFill')[0];

      if (!pf || pf.getAttribute('patternType') !== 'solid') return null;

      const fg = pf.getElementsByTagName('fgColor')[0];

      return fg ? fg.getAttribute('rgb') : null;

    });

    const xfsNode = stylesDoc.getElementsByTagName('cellXfs')[0];

    styleFill = (xfsNode ? [...xfsNode.getElementsByTagName('xf')] : []).map(xf =>

      fillRgb[parseInt(xf.getAttribute('fillId') || '0', 10)] || null);

  }


  const rowsData = [];

  for (const rowEl of sheetDoc.getElementsByTagName('row')) {

    const rowMap = {};

    for (const c of rowEl.getElementsByTagName('c')) {

      const colIdx = colLetterToIndex(c.getAttribute('r'));

      const t = c.getAttribute('t');

      const sIdx = c.getAttribute('s');

      const vEl = c.getElementsByTagName('v')[0];

      let value = null;

      if (t === 's') {

        if (vEl) value = sharedStrings[parseInt(vEl.textContent, 10)] ?? null;

      } else if (t === 'inlineStr') {

        const isEl = c.getElementsByTagName('is')[0];

        if (isEl) value = [...isEl.getElementsByTagName('t')].map(x => x.textContent).join('');

      } else if (vEl) {

        const num = parseFloat(vEl.textContent);

        value = isNaN(num) ? vEl.textContent : num;

      }

      const rgb = sIdx != null ? (styleFill[parseInt(sIdx, 10)] || null) : null;

      rowMap[colIdx] = { value, rgb };

    }

    rowsData.push(rowMap);

  }

  return { rowsData, usedFallback, sheetName: target.getAttribute('name') || '' };

}


/* Walk the raw grid into { date: { dow, entries } }, given the calendar

   year the first row's month belongs to. Pure and synchronous, so the

   preview can re-run instantly whenever the year field is corrected. */

function datesFromGrid(rowsData, startYear) {

  let year = startYear, monthNum = null, curDate = null;

  const out = {};

  for (const row of rowsData) {

    const a = row[1];

    if (a && typeof a.value === 'string' && MONTH_NUM[a.value.trim()]) {

      const m = MONTH_NUM[a.value.trim()];

      if (monthNum !== null && m < monthNum) year += 1;

      monthNum = m;

    }

    const day = row[2] ? row[2].value : null;

    const name = row[5] ? row[5].value : null;


    if (day != null && monthNum != null) {

      curDate = `${year}-${String(monthNum).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(Math.round(day)).padStart(2, '0')}`;

    } else if (name == null) {

      curDate = null;

    }

    if (!name || curDate == null) continue;


    const rgb = row[4] ? row[4].rgb : null;

    const color = rgb ? XLSX_FILL_TO_NAME[rgb] : undefined;

    const entry = { name: String(name).trim() };

    if (row[6] && row[6].value) entry.readings = String(row[6].value).trim();

    if (row[7] && row[7].value) entry.comm = String(row[7].value).trim();

    if (color) entry.color = color;


    out[curDate] = out[curDate] || { dow: row[3] ? row[3].value : null, entries: [] };

    out[curDate].entries.push(entry);

  }

  return out;

}


function litOn(dateISO) {

  const d = App.liturgical[dateISO];

  if (!d || !d.entries || !d.entries.length) return null;

  return d;

}

/* First entry's name plus a "+N" marker when a day carries more than one proper

   (Christmas Day, Palm Sunday, and similar have separate Masses/Offices listed). */

function litSummary(dateISO) {

  const d = litOn(dateISO);

  if (!d) return null;

  const first = d.entries[0];

  return { name: first.name, color: first.color || null, extra: d.entries.length - 1,

           all: d.entries };

}


const fmtTime = t => {

  const [h, m] = t.split(':').map(Number);

  return `${((h + 11) % 12) + 1}:${String(m).padStart(2,'0')} ${h < 12 ? 'am' : 'pm'}`;

};

const fmtDateLong = dateISO => {

  const d = fromISO(dateISO);

  return `${MONTHS[d.getMonth()].slice(0, 3)} ${d.getDate()}, ${d.getFullYear()}`;

};

const el = id => document.getElementById(id);

const esc = s => String(s == null ? '' : s)

  .replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');


/* ---------- configuration ----------

   Fill these in once, after running supabase-setup.sql in your Supabase project

   (Project Settings → API → Project URL / anon public key). They are not secret —

   Supabase's security comes from the database rules in that SQL file, not from

   hiding these values. Every visitor still needs the shared password to do anything. */

const SUPABASE_URL = 'https://dteoeqtozzeuywkxzpny.supabase.co';

const SUPABASE_ANON_KEY = 'sb_publishable_3BAnU28Q6AJ8R3iKqSe6sA_u5POPHos';


/* ---------- storage (a real database, shared across every device) ----------

   Every call is checked against the password server-side, inside the database

   itself (see supabase-setup.sql) — not just by this page's own JavaScript.

   That matters because anyone can view this page's source; the actual gate has

   to live somewhere they can't bypass by doing that. */

let sbClient = null;

let storageLive = false;

let authFailure = null; // set when the server rejects our password specifically


function configured() {

  return SUPABASE_URL && SUPABASE_ANON_KEY &&

    !SUPABASE_URL.includes('YOUR_SUPABASE') && !SUPABASE_ANON_KEY.includes('YOUR_SUPABASE');

}


function getCachedPassword() {

  try { return localStorage.getItem('roster_password') || ''; }

  catch (e) { return ''; }

}

function setCachedPassword(pw) {

  try { localStorage.setItem('roster_password', pw); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }

}

function clearCachedPassword() {

  try { localStorage.removeItem('roster_password'); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }

}


function isWrongPasswordError(msg) {

  return /incorrect password/i.test(msg || '');

}


/* Turns a raw Supabase error into something a person can actually act on.

   The exact wording Supabase/PostgREST use for each of these is stable enough

   to match on, and knowing *which* of these it is changes what to fix. */

function describeConnectionError(e) {

  const msg = (e && e.message) || String(e || 'Unknown error');

  const code = e && e.code;

  if (code === '42883' || /could not find the function|schema cache|does not exist/i.test(msg)) {

    return `The database doesn't have the expected setup yet. Supabase said: "${msg}" — ` +

      `open supabase-setup.sql and confirm the whole file ran with no errors in the SQL Editor.`;

  }

  if (/failed to fetch|networkerror|econnrefused|fetch failed|name not resolved/i.test(msg)) {

    return `Couldn't reach that address at all. Check SUPABASE_URL near the top of the file — ` +

      `it should look like https://xxxxxxxx.supabase.co, with no extra spaces, quotes, or ` +

      `trailing slash.`;

  }

  if (/invalid api key|jwt|apikey/i.test(msg)) {

    return `The database rejected the key this page is using. Supabase said: "${msg}" — check ` +

      `SUPABASE_ANON_KEY is the "anon public" key from Project Settings → API (not the ` +

      `service_role key, and not the Project URL pasted into the wrong field).`;

  }

  return `Supabase said: "${msg}"`;

}


async function checkPassword(pw) {

  const { data, error } = await sbClient.rpc('kv_check_password', { p_password: pw });

  if (error) throw error;

  return data === true;

}


const Store = {

  async get(key) {

    try {

      const { data, error } = await sbClient.rpc('kv_get',

        { p_password: getCachedPassword(), p_key: key });

      storageLive = true;

      if (error) {

        if (isWrongPasswordError(error.message)) authFailure = error.message;

        return null;

      }

      return data ?? null;

    } catch (e) {

      storageLive = true;

      return null;

    }

  },

  async set(key, val) {

    const { data, error } = await sbClient.rpc('kv_set',

      { p_password: getCachedPassword(), p_key: key, p_value: val });

    storageLive = true;

    if (error) {

      if (isWrongPasswordError(error.message)) authFailure = error.message;

      throw error;

    }

    return data;

  },

};

const yearKey = y => `roster:year:${y}`;

const SETTINGS_KEY = 'roster:settings';

const LITURGICAL_KEY = 'roster:liturgical';


/* ---------- state ---------- */

const App = {

  view: 'week',

  cursor: new Date(),

  services: [],                 // every service for every loaded year

  loadedYears: new Set(),

  liturgical: { ...LITURGICAL_BUILTIN },   // working calendar; a saved import overrides this

  liturgicalStamp: 0,

  stamps: {},                   // year → updatedAt of last read/write

  settings: { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, parishName: 'All Saints Lutheran Church & School' },

  dirtyYears: new Set(),

  saveTimer: null,

  lastDeleted: null,

  booted: false,

};


const setSync = (s, txt) => { const n = el('sync'); n.dataset.s = s; n.textContent = txt || ''; };

let toastTimer;

function toast(msg, ms = 3200) {

  const t = el('toast');

  t.innerHTML = msg;

  t.classList.add('show');

  clearTimeout(toastTimer);

  toastTimer = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), ms);

}


/* ---------- load / save ---------- */

async function ensureYears(years) {

  for (const y of years) {

    if (App.loadedYears.has(y)) continue;

    const rec = await Store.get(yearKey(y));

    App.loadedYears.add(y);

    App.stamps[y] = rec ? rec.updatedAt || 0 : 0;

    if (rec && Array.isArray(rec.services)) {

      const have = new Set(App.services.map(s => s.id));

      rec.services.forEach(s => { if (!have.has(s.id)) App.services.push(s); });

    }

  }

  App.services.sort((a, b) => (a.date + a.time).localeCompare(b.date + b.time));

}


function markDirty(years) {

  years.forEach(y => App.dirtyYears.add(y));

  setSync('saving', 'saving…');

  clearTimeout(App.saveTimer);

  App.saveTimer = setTimeout(flush, 650);

}


async function flush() {

  const years = [...App.dirtyYears];

  App.dirtyYears.clear();

  if (!years.length) return;

  try {

    for (const y of years) {

      const stamp = Date.now();

      await Store.set(yearKey(y), {

        updatedAt: stamp,

        services: App.services.filter(s => s.date.startsWith(String(y))),

      });

      App.stamps[y] = stamp;

    }

    setSync('idle', 'saved');

  } catch (e) {

    setSync('error', 'not saved');

    toast('Could not save. Your last change is still on screen — try again in a moment.');

  }

}


async function saveSettings() {

  try {

    await Store.set(SETTINGS_KEY, { updatedAt: Date.now(), settings: App.settings });

    setSync('idle', 'saved');

  } catch (e) { setSync('error', 'not saved'); }

}


/* The liturgical calendar persists the same way the schedule does, so an

   import made by one person appears for everyone else too. */

async function saveLiturgical() {

  const stamp = Date.now();

  await Store.set(LITURGICAL_KEY, { updatedAt: stamp, calendar: App.liturgical });

  App.liturgicalStamp = stamp;

  setSync('idle', 'saved');

}


/* Someone else may be editing. Pull their changes in when we have none pending. */

async function poll() {

  if (App.dirtyYears.size) return;

  const years = visibleYears();

  let changed = false;

  for (const y of years) {

    const rec = await Store.get(yearKey(y));

    const stamp = rec ? rec.updatedAt || 0 : 0;

    if (stamp > (App.stamps[y] || 0)) {

      App.services = App.services.filter(s => !s.date.startsWith(String(y)));

      (rec.services || []).forEach(s => App.services.push(s));

      App.stamps[y] = stamp;

      changed = true;

    }

  }

  const litRec = await Store.get(LITURGICAL_KEY);

  const litStamp = litRec ? litRec.updatedAt || 0 : 0;

  if (litRec && litRec.calendar && litStamp > App.liturgicalStamp) {

    App.liturgical = litRec.calendar;

    App.liturgicalStamp = litStamp;

    changed = true;

  }

  if (changed) {

    App.services.sort((a, b) => (a.date + a.time).localeCompare(b.date + b.time));

    render();

    toast('Schedule updated by someone else.');

  }

}


/* ---------- selectors ---------- */

const live = () => App.services.filter(s => !s.deleted);

const onDate = d => live().filter(s => s.date === d).sort((a,b) => a.time.localeCompare(b.time));

const removedOn = ds => App.services.filter(s => s.deleted && ds.includes(s.date));


function visibleYears() {

  const c = App.cursor;

  if (App.view === 'year') return [c.getFullYear()];

  if (App.view === 'month') {

    const first = new Date(c.getFullYear(), c.getMonth(), 1);

    return [...new Set([startOfWeek(first).getFullYear(),

      addDays(startOfWeek(new Date(c.getFullYear(), c.getMonth() + 1, 0)), 6).getFullYear()])];

  }

  const s = startOfWeek(c);

  return [...new Set([s.getFullYear(), addDays(s, 6).getFullYear()])];

}


/* Preacher is always "P", Deacon is always "Deac.", and Subdeacon (used only

   on the rare high-feast service that needs a third minister) is always

   "Sub.D." — none of those depend on which service it is. The Celebrant/

   Liturgist role is the one exception: he's the Celebrant specifically at

   Divine Service, and the Liturgist everywhere else (Matins, Evening Prayer,

   and any added feast-day service that isn't itself a Divine Service). */

function roleShort(role, serviceName) {

  if (role === 'celebrant') {

    return serviceName && serviceName.startsWith('Divine Service') ? 'Cel.' : 'Lit.';

  }

  if (role === 'deacon') return 'Deac.';

  if (role === 'subdeacon') return 'Sub.D.';

  if (role === 'preacher') return 'P';

  return ROLE_SHORT[role] || role;

}


function menOf(s) {

  const order = [], map = {};

  for (const r of s.roles) {

    const p = s.assign && s.assign[r];

    if (!p) continue;

    if (!map[p]) { map[p] = []; order.push(p); }

    map[p].push(r);

  }

  return order.map(p => ({ p, roleKeys: map[p] }));

}

const openRoles = s => s.roles.filter(r => !(s.assign && s.assign[r]));


function chip(p, roleKeys, serviceName) {

  const man = PMAP[p];

  const named = roleKeys.map(r => ROLE_LABEL[r]);

  const title = named.length ? `${man.name} — ${named.join(', ')}` : man.name;

  const shortText = roleKeys.map(r => roleShort(r, serviceName)).join('/');

  return `<span class="chip" data-p="${p}" title="${esc(title)}">` +

         `<span class="who">${p}</span>` +

         (roleKeys.length ? `<span class="rl">${esc(shortText)}</span>` : '') + `</span>`;

}

function chipsFor(s) {

  let h = menOf(s).map(m => chip(m.p, m.roleKeys, s.name)).join('');

  const open = openRoles(s);

  if (open.length) {

    const shortText = open.map(r => roleShort(r, s.name)).join('/');

    h += `<span class="chip gap" title="Unassigned: ${esc(open.map(r => ROLE_LABEL[r]).join(', '))}">` +

         `<span class="who">?</span><span class="rl">${esc(shortText)}</span></span>`;

  }

  return h;

}


/* Which dates have rule problems, and what are they */

function issuesByDate() {

  const scope = live();

  const map = new Map();

  checkRules(scope, App.settings).forEach(i => {

    const k = i.date || 'range';

    if (!map.has(k)) map.set(k, []);

    map.get(k).push(i.msg);

  });

  return map;

}


/* ---------- rendering ---------- */

function render() {

  el('parishName').textContent = App.settings.parishName || 'Pastoral Roster';

  document.querySelectorAll('#tabs button').forEach(b =>

    b.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(b.dataset.v === App.view)));

  const c = App.cursor;

  el('period').textContent = App.view === 'year' ? c.getFullYear()

    : App.view === 'month' ? `${MONTHS[c.getMonth()]} ${c.getFullYear()}`

    : (() => {

        const a2 = startOfWeek(c), b2 = addDays(a2, 6);

        return a2.getMonth() === b2.getMonth()

          ? `${MONTHS[a2.getMonth()].slice(0,3)} ${a2.getDate()}–${b2.getDate()}`

          : `${MONTHS[a2.getMonth()].slice(0,3)} ${a2.getDate()} – ` +

            `${MONTHS[b2.getMonth()].slice(0,3)} ${b2.getDate()}`;

      })();

  renderBanner();

  if (App.view === 'week') renderWeek();

  else if (App.view === 'month') renderMonth();

  else renderYear();

  renderLegend();

}


function renderBanner() {

  const b = el('banner');

  const empty = live().length === 0;

  let h = '';

  if (!configured()) {

    h += `<div class="banner"><b>This roster isn't connected to a database yet.</b>

          Open this file in a text editor and fill in SUPABASE_URL and SUPABASE_ANON_KEY

          near the top of the script — see supabase-setup.sql for how to get them.</div>`;

  } else if (authFailure) {

    h += `<div class="banner"><b>The saved password stopped working</b> — it may have

          been changed. <button class="tinybtn" id="reAuthBtn">Enter it again</button></div>`;

  }

  if (empty) {

    h += `<div class="banner"><b>No services yet.</b> Choose <b>Fill schedule</b> to build a

          year from the parish rules, then adjust anything by hand.</div>`;

  }

  b.innerHTML = h;

  const reBtn = el('reAuthBtn');

  if (reBtn) reBtn.onclick = () => { clearCachedPassword(); showGate(); };

}


/* ----- week ----- */

function renderWeek() {

  const start = startOfWeek(App.cursor);

  const days = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => addDays(start, i));

  const todayISO = iso(new Date());

  const issues = issuesByDate();

  let h = `<div class="sect-h"><h2>Week of ${MONTHS[start.getMonth()]} ${start.getDate()}</h2>

    <span class="note">Change any dropdown to override. Overridden services are kept when you

    refill the schedule.</span></div><div class="wk">`;


  for (const d of days) {

    const ds = iso(d), list = onDate(ds), isToday = ds === todayISO;

    const feast = litSummary(ds);

    const gut = n => `<div class="gut${isToday ? ' today' : ''}">${n ? `

        <div class="dw">${DOW_SHORT[d.getDay()]}</div><div class="dn">${d.getDate()}</div>

        ${feast ? `<div class="lit" title="${esc(feast.all.map(x =>

            x.readings ? `${x.name}: ${x.readings}` : x.name).join(' · '))}">

          ${feast.color ? `<span class="sw" style="background:${LIT_COLOR[feast.color]}"></span>` : ''}

          <span class="lname">${esc(feast.name)}${feast.extra ? ` +${feast.extra}` : ''}</span>

        </div>` : ''}` : ''}</div>`;


    if (!list.length) {

      h += `<div class="row dim">${gut(true)}<div class="body">

        <div class="hd"><span class="nm" style="color:var(--mute);font-size:14px">No services</span>

        <span class="rowacts"><button class="tinybtn" data-add="${ds}">Add one</button></span></div>

      </div></div>`;

    } else {

      list.forEach((s, i) => {

        h += `<div class="row">${gut(i === 0)}<div class="body">

          <div class="hd">

            <span class="nm">${esc(s.name)}</span>

            <span class="tm">${fmtTime(s.time)}</span>

            ${s.special ? '<span class="tag">Added</span>' : ''}

            ${s.locked ? '<span class="tag">Kept</span>' : ''}

            <span class="rowacts">

              <button class="tinybtn${s.locked ? ' on' : ''}" data-lock="${s.id}"

                title="Keep this service exactly as it is when the schedule is refilled">

                ${s.locked ? 'Kept' : 'Keep'}</button>

              <button class="tinybtn" data-del="${s.id}">Remove</button>

            </span>

          </div>

          <div class="slots">${s.roles.map(r => {

            const cur = (s.assign && s.assign[r]) || '';

            return `<div class="slot" data-p="${cur}">

              <label for="sel-${s.id}-${r}">${ROLE_LABEL[r]}</label>

              <select id="sel-${s.id}-${r}" data-svc="${s.id}" data-role="${r}">

                <option value=""${cur ? '' : ' selected'}>— not assigned —</option>

                ${PASTORS.map(p => `<option value="${p.id}"${cur === p.id ? ' selected' : ''}>

                  ${p.name}</option>`).join('')}

              </select></div>`;

          }).join('')}</div>

          <div class="notewrap">

            <textarea class="noteinput${s.notes ? ' has' : ''}" data-svc="${s.id}"

              placeholder="Add a note for the team…" rows="1">${esc(s.notes || '')}</textarea>

          </div>`;

        if (i === list.length - 1 && issues.has(ds)) {

          h += issues.get(ds).map(m =>

            `<div class="warn"><b>Check</b><span>${esc(m)}</span></div>`).join('');

        }

        h += `</div></div>`;

      });

    }

  }

  const gone = removedOn(days.map(iso));

  if (gone.length) {

    h += `<div class="restore">${gone.length} service${gone.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} removed

      this week. <button class="tinybtn" data-restoreweek="1">Put back</button></div>`;

  }

  h += `</div>`;

  el('view').innerHTML = h;

  document.querySelectorAll('.noteinput').forEach(ta => {

    ta.style.height = 'auto';

    ta.style.height = ta.scrollHeight + 'px';

  });

}


/* ----- month ----- */

function renderMonth() {

  const c = App.cursor;

  const first = new Date(c.getFullYear(), c.getMonth(), 1);

  const gridStart = startOfWeek(first);

  const todayISO = iso(new Date());

  const issues = issuesByDate();

  let h = `<div class="sect-h"><h2>${MONTHS[c.getMonth()]} ${c.getFullYear()}</h2>

    <span class="note">Initials name the pastor; the letters after them are his roles.

    Choose any service to edit it.</span></div><div class="grid">` +

    DOW_SHORT.map(d => `<div class="dow">${d}</div>`).join('');


  for (let i = 0; i < 42; i++) {

    const d = addDays(gridStart, i);

    if (i >= 35 && d.getMonth() !== c.getMonth()) break;

    const ds = iso(d), out = d.getMonth() !== c.getMonth(), list = onDate(ds);

    const bad = issues.has(ds);

    const feast = litSummary(ds);

    const litStyle = feast && feast.color ? ` style="--lc:${LIT_COLOR[feast.color]}"` : '';

    h += `<div class="cell${out ? ' out' : ''}${ds === todayISO ? ' today' : ''}${bad ? ' bad' : ''}

      ${feast && feast.color ? ' lit' : ''}"${litStyle}

      ${bad ? `title="${esc(issues.get(ds).join(' · '))}"` : ''}>

      <div class="dhd"><div class="dnum">${d.getDate()}<span class="dwm">${DOW_LONG[d.getDay()]}</span></div></div>

      ${feast ? `<div class="feast" title="${esc(feast.all.map(x => x.name).join(' · '))}">

        ${esc(feast.name)}${feast.extra ? `<span class="more"> +${feast.extra}</span>` : ''}</div>` : ''}`;

    for (const s of list) {

      h += `<div class="svc${s.special ? ' spc' : ''}" data-goto="${ds}">

        <div class="t"><span class="nm">${esc(s.name)}</span><span>${fmtTime(s.time)}</span></div>

        <div class="men">${chipsFor(s)}</div></div>`;

    }

    if (!list.length && !out) {

      h += `<div class="noSvc">No service <button class="addln" data-add="${ds}">+ add</button></div>`;

    }

    h += `</div>`;

  }

  h += `</div>`;

  el('view').innerHTML = h;

}


/* ----- year ----- */

function renderYear() {

  const y = App.cursor.getFullYear();

  const yes = live().filter(s => s.date.startsWith(String(y)));

  const t = tally(yes);

  const eff = effectiveTargets(App.settings);

  const stated = (() => {

    const w = App.settings.weights;

    const sum = PIDS.reduce((a, p) => a + (w[p] || 0), 0) || 1;

    return Object.fromEntries(PIDS.map(p => [p, (w[p] || 0) / sum]));

  })();

  const issues = checkRules(yes, App.settings);


  const ledger = PASTORS.map(p => {

    const n = t.byPastor[p.id].preacher;

    const pct = t.sermons ? n / t.sermons : 0;

    return `<div class="r"><div class="top">

        <span class="n"><span class="swatch" style="background:${p.color}"></span>${p.last}</span>

        <span class="v"><b>${(pct*100).toFixed(1)}%</b> &middot; ${n} sermon${n===1?'':'s'}

          &middot; aim ${(stated[p.id]*100).toFixed(0)}%</span></div>

      <div class="track">

        <div class="fill" style="width:${Math.min(100, pct*100).toFixed(1)}%;background:${p.color}"></div>

        <div class="tgt" style="left:${Math.min(99.5, stated[p.id]*100).toFixed(1)}%"

          title="Aim: ${(stated[p.id]*100).toFixed(0)}%"></div>

      </div></div>`;

  }).join('');


  const conflict = Math.abs(eff.LK - stated.LK) > 0.001;

  const dutyRows = PASTORS.map(p => {

    const x = t.byPastor[p.id];

    return `<tr><td><span class="swatch" style="background:${p.color}"></span>${p.last}</td>

      <td>${x.celebrant}</td><td>${x.deacon}</td><td>${x.preacher}</td><td>${x.total}</td></tr>`;

  }).join('');


  const checks = issues.length

    ? issues.slice(0, 60).map(i => `<div class="iss">

        ${i.date ? `<span class="d">${i.date}</span>` : ''}${esc(i.msg)}</div>`).join('')

    : `<div class="ok">&#10003; Every service in ${y} follows the parish rules.</div>`;


  let minis = '';

  for (let m = 0; m < 12; m++) {

    const first = new Date(y, m, 1), gs = startOfWeek(first);

    const count = yes.filter(s => s.date.startsWith(`${y}-${String(m+1).padStart(2,'0')}`)).length;

    let cells = DOW_SHORT.map(d => `<div class="h">${d[0]}</div>`).join('');

    for (let i = 0; i < 42; i++) {

      const d = addDays(gs, i);

      if (i >= 35 && d.getMonth() !== m) break;

      if (d.getMonth() !== m) { cells += `<div class="d e"></div>`; continue; }

      const dots = onDate(iso(d)).map(s => {

        const p = (s.assign && (s.assign.preacher || s.assign.celebrant)) || null;

        return p ? `<span class="dot" style="background:${PMAP[p].color}"></span>` : '';

      }).join('');

      cells += `<div class="d">${dots}</div>`;

    }

    minis += `<button class="mini" data-month="${m}">

      <h4>${MONTHS[m]}<span>${count}</span></h4><div class="mgrid">${cells}</div></button>`;

  }


  el('view').innerHTML = `

    <div class="sect-h"><h2>${y}</h2>

      <span class="note">${yes.length} services &middot; ${t.sermons} sermons &middot;

      dots mark who preaches. Choose a month to open it.</span></div>

    <div class="ycols">

      <div>

        <div class="panel">

          <h3>Preaching</h3>

          <p class="sub">Bars are what the year actually holds. The upright mark is the aim.</p>

          <div class="led">${ledger}</div>

          ${conflict ? `<div class="banner" style="margin:14px 0 0">

            <b>Two rules pull against each other.</b> Fr. Leckband at 10% means a sermon every

            other week, but the six-week interval allows ${(eff.LK*100).toFixed(1)}%. The interval

            is winning, and his remaining share is shared out among the others. Change either one

            in Settings.</div>` : ''}

        </div>

        <div class="panel" style="margin-top:16px">

          <h3>Duties</h3>

          <p class="sub">Every appointment across the year.</p>

          <table class="dtable"><thead><tr><th>Pastor</th><th>Celeb</th><th>Deacon</th>

            <th>Preach</th><th>All</th></tr></thead><tbody>${dutyRows}</tbody></table>

        </div>

        <div class="panel" style="margin-top:16px">

          <h3>Rule check</h3>

          <p class="sub">Run continuously, including on anything edited by hand.</p>

          <div class="checks">${checks}</div>

        </div>

      </div>

      <div><div class="minis">${minis}</div></div>

    </div>`;

}


function renderLegend() {

  el('legend').innerHTML =

    `<div class="grp"><span class="k">Pastors</span>${PASTORS.map(p =>

      `<span class="who">${chip(p.id, [])}${p.name}</span>`).join('')}</div>

     <div class="grp"><span class="k">Roles</span>

       <span>Cel. — Celebrant (Divine Service)</span>

       <span>Lit. — Liturgist (Matins, Evening Prayer)</span>

       <span>Deac. — Deacon</span>

       <span>Sub.D. — Subdeacon (rare, added feasts)</span>

       <span>P — Preacher</span></div>

     <div class="grp"><span class="k">Liturgical color</span>${Object.keys(LIT_COLOR).map(c =>

       `<span class="who"><span class="swatch" style="background:${LIT_COLOR[c]}"></span>${LIT_LABEL[c]}</span>`).join('')}

       <span style="color:var(--mute-2)">— The Lutheran Missal, 2025–2027</span></div>

     <div class="grp"><span class="k">Sharing</span>

       <span>Everyone opening this roster sees and edits the same schedule.</span></div>`;

}


/* ---------- editing ---------- */

function svc(id) { return App.services.find(s => s.id === id); }

const yearOf = s => Number(s.date.slice(0, 4));


function onChangeAssign(id, role, val) {

  const s = svc(id);

  if (!s) return;

  s.assign = s.assign || {};

  s.assign[role] = val || null;

  s.locked = true;

  markDirty([yearOf(s)]);

  render();

}


function toggleLock(id) {

  const s = svc(id);

  s.locked = !s.locked;

  markDirty([yearOf(s)]);

  render();

}


function removeService(id) {

  const s = svc(id);

  if (!s) return;

  if (s.special) App.services = App.services.filter(x => x.id !== id);

  else { s.deleted = true; s.locked = true; }

  App.lastDeleted = id;

  markDirty([yearOf(s)]);

  render();

  toast(`Removed ${esc(s.name)} on ${s.date}. <a href="#" id="undoDel"

    style="color:var(--brass-lt)">Undo</a>`, 6000);

  setTimeout(() => {

    const u = el('undoDel');

    if (u) u.onclick = e => { e.preventDefault(); undoDelete(s); };

  }, 0);

}


function undoDelete(snapshot) {

  if (snapshot.special) App.services.push(snapshot);

  else { const s = svc(snapshot.id); if (s) { s.deleted = false; } }

  markDirty([yearOf(snapshot)]);

  render();

  toast('Put back.');

}


/* ---------- dialogs ---------- */

function closeAll() {

  el('scrim').classList.remove('open');

  el('drawer').classList.remove('open');

  el('dlg').classList.remove('open');

}

function openDlg(title, body, foot) {

  el('dlgTitle').textContent = title;

  el('dlgBody').innerHTML = body;

  el('dlgFoot').innerHTML = foot;

  el('scrim').classList.add('open');

  el('dlg').classList.add('open');

}


/* ----- add a service ----- */

function openAdd(dateISO) {

  const d = dateISO || iso(App.cursor);

  openDlg('Add a service', `

    <div class="grp2">

      <div class="fld"><label for="asDate">Date</label>

        <input type="date" id="asDate" value="${d}"></div>

      <div class="fld"><label for="asTime">Time</label>

        <input type="time" id="asTime" value="18:30"></div>

    </div>

    <div class="lit-suggest" id="asLit"></div>

    <div class="fld"><label for="asName">Service</label>

      <input type="text" id="asName" list="svcNames" value="Divine Service — Feast">

      <datalist id="svcNames">${SERVICE_NAMES.map(n => `<option value="${esc(n)}">`).join('')}</datalist>

    </div>

    <div class="fld"><label>Roles to fill</label>

      <div class="rolechk">

        ${ROLES.map(r => `<label><input type="checkbox" class="asRole" value="${r}"

          ${(r === 'deacon' || r === 'subdeacon') ? '' : 'checked'}>${ROLE_LABEL[r]}</label>`).join('')}

      </div>

      <div class="hint">Assign the men after you add it, in the week view.</div>

    </div>`,

    `<button class="btn2" data-close>Cancel</button>

     <button class="btn2 gold" id="asSave">Add service</button>`);


  const refreshLit = () => {

    const box = el('asLit');

    const feast = litSummary(el('asDate').value);

    if (!feast) { box.classList.remove('show'); box.innerHTML = ''; return; }

    const first = feast.all[0];

    box.classList.add('show');

    box.innerHTML =

      `${feast.color ? `<span class="sw" style="background:${LIT_COLOR[feast.color]}"></span>` : ''}

       <span><b>${esc(first.name)}</b>${feast.extra ? ` &middot; +${feast.extra} more this day` : ''}

       ${first.readings ? `<div class="rd">${esc(first.readings)}</div>` : ''}

       <button type="button" id="asUseFeast">Use this as the service name</button></span>`;

    const btn = el('asUseFeast');

    if (btn) btn.onclick = () => { el('asName').value = first.name; };

  };

  el('asDate').addEventListener('change', refreshLit);

  refreshLit();


  el('asSave').onclick = () => {

    const date = el('asDate').value, time = el('asTime').value;

    const name = el('asName').value.trim() || 'Service';

    const roles = [...document.querySelectorAll('.asRole:checked')].map(c => c.value);

    if (!date || !time || !roles.length) {

      toast('A date, a time, and at least one role are needed.'); return;

    }

    const y = Number(date.slice(0, 4));

    ensureYears([y]).then(() => {

      App.services.push({

        id: `sp-${date}-${time.replace(':','')}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7)}`,

        date, time, name, roles,

        assign: Object.fromEntries(roles.map(r => [r, null])),

        locked: true, special: true,

      });

      App.services.sort((a,b) => (a.date+a.time).localeCompare(b.date+b.time));

      markDirty([y]);

      App.cursor = fromISO(date);

      App.view = 'week';

      closeAll(); render();

      toast(`Added ${esc(name)} on ${date}. Assign the men below.`);

    });

  };

}


/* ----- fill schedule ----- */

function openGenerate() {

  const y = App.cursor.getFullYear(), m = App.cursor.getMonth();

  openDlg('Fill schedule', `

    <div class="fld"><label>Range</label>

      <select id="gnScope">

        <option value="year">All of ${y}</option>

        <option value="month">${MONTHS[m]} ${y} only</option>

        <option value="rest">${MONTHS[m]} ${y} to the end of the year</option>

      </select>

    </div>

    <div class="fld"><label>What happens</label>

      <div class="hint">The weekly pattern is written across the range and the men are assigned

      from the parish rules. Anything you marked <b>Kept</b>, anything you added by hand, and

      anything you removed all stay as they are. Everything else is rewritten.</div>

    </div>`,

    `<button class="btn2" data-close>Cancel</button>

     <button class="btn2 gold" id="gnGo">Fill it</button>`);

  el('gnGo').onclick = async () => {

    const scope = el('gnScope').value;

    let a, b;

    if (scope === 'year') { a = `${y}-01-01`; b = `${y}-12-31`; }

    else if (scope === 'month') {

      a = iso(new Date(y, m, 1)); b = iso(new Date(y, m + 1, 0));

    } else { a = iso(new Date(y, m, 1)); b = `${y}-12-31`; }

    closeAll();

    setSync('saving', 'filling…');

    await ensureYears([y]);

    const others = App.services.filter(s => !s.date.startsWith(String(y)));

    const mine = App.services.filter(s => s.date.startsWith(String(y)));

    const built = generateRange(a, b, App.settings, mine);

    App.services = [...others, ...built]

      .sort((x, z) => (x.date + x.time).localeCompare(z.date + z.time));

    markDirty([y]);

    render();

    const n = built.filter(s => !s.deleted).length;

    toast(`Filled ${n} services. Check the rule panel in the year view.`);

  };

}


/* ----- export ----- */

function csvFor(list) {

  const head = ['Date','Day','Time','Service','Celebrant/Liturgist','Deacon','Subdeacon','Preacher','Status'];

  const q = v => `"${String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/"/g,'""')}"`;

  const rows = list.map(s => {

    const nm = r => {

      if (!s.roles.includes(r)) return '';

      const p = s.assign && s.assign[r];

      return p ? PMAP[p].name : 'unassigned';

    };

    return [s.date, DOW_LONG[fromISO(s.date).getDay()], fmtTime(s.time), s.name,

      nm('celebrant'), nm('deacon'), nm('subdeacon'), nm('preacher'),

      [s.special ? 'added' : '', s.locked ? 'kept' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ')].map(q).join(',');

  });

  return [head.map(q).join(','), ...rows].join('\r\n');

}


function openExport() {

  const y = App.cursor.getFullYear(), m = App.cursor.getMonth();

  openDlg('Export to a spreadsheet', `

    <div class="fld"><label>Range</label>

      <select id="exScope">

        <option value="month">${MONTHS[m]} ${y}</option>

        <option value="year" selected>All of ${y}</option>

        <option value="all">Every year on file</option>

      </select></div>

    <div class="fld"><label>Format</label>

      <div class="hint">A CSV file, which opens directly in Excel, Numbers, or Google Sheets.</div>

    </div>

    <div id="exFallback"></div>`,

    `<button class="btn2" data-close>Cancel</button>

     <button class="btn2" id="exShow">Show the text</button>

     <button class="btn2 gold" id="exGo">Download</button>`);


  const pick = () => {

    const s = el('exScope').value;

    if (s === 'all') return live();

    if (s === 'year') return live().filter(x => x.date.startsWith(String(y)));

    return live().filter(x => x.date.startsWith(`${y}-${String(m+1).padStart(2,'0')}`));

  };

  const nameFor = () => {

    const s = el('exScope').value;

    return s === 'all' ? 'roster-all-years.csv'

      : s === 'year' ? `roster-${y}.csv`

      : `roster-${y}-${String(m+1).padStart(2,'0')}.csv`;

  };

  el('exGo').onclick = () => {

    const list = pick();

    if (!list.length) { toast('Nothing in that range yet.'); return; }

    const csv = csvFor(list);

    try {

      const blob = new Blob(['\ufeff' + csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' });

      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

      const a = document.createElement('a');

      a.href = url; a.download = nameFor();

      document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();

      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1500);

      toast(`Exported ${list.length} services.`);

    } catch (e) { showText(csv); }

  };

  const showText = csv => {

    el('exFallback').innerHTML = `<div class="fld"><label>Copy this into a spreadsheet</label>

      <textarea class="csv" id="exText" readonly></textarea></div>`;

    el('exText').value = csv;

    el('exText').select();

  };

  el('exShow').onclick = () => {

    const list = pick();

    if (!list.length) { toast('Nothing in that range yet.'); return; }

    showText(csvFor(list));

  };

}


/* ----- settings ----- */

function openSettings() {

  const s = App.settings;

  const W = startOfWeek(App.cursor);

  const wi = weekIndexOf(W);

  const curSun = (wi + s.alternationAnchor) % 2 === 0 ? 'LG' : 'SU';

  el('setBody').innerHTML = `

    <div class="fld"><label for="stName">Roster name</label>

      <input type="text" id="stName" value="${esc(s.parishName || '')}"

        placeholder="All Saints Lutheran Church & School"></div>


    <div class="divider-l">Preaching share</div>

    ${PASTORS.map(p => `<div class="wrow">

      <span><span class="swatch" style="background:${p.color}"></span>${p.name}</span>

      <input type="number" min="0" max="100" step="1" class="stW" data-p="${p.id}"

        value="${s.weights[p.id]}"></div>`).join('')}

    <div class="hint" style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--mute)">

      Percentages. They are treated as proportions, so they need not total 100.</div>


    <div class="divider-l">Limits</div>

    <div class="grp2">

      <div class="fld"><label for="stLk">Weeks between Leckband sermons</label>

        <input type="number" min="1" max="26" id="stLk" value="${s.leckbandInterval}"></div>

      <div class="fld"><label for="stMax">Sermons per man per week</label>

        <input type="number" min="1" max="5" id="stMax" value="${s.maxSermonsPerWeek}"></div>

    </div>

    <label class="swtch"><input type="checkbox" id="stCap" ${s.capLeckbandToInterval ? 'checked' : ''}>

      <span>Hold Fr. Leckband to that interval

      <span class="sub">On: the interval wins and his unused share goes to the others.

      Off: his percentage wins and he preaches more often.</span></span></label>

    <label class="swtch"><input type="checkbox" id="stEp" ${s.eveningPrayerPrefersJuniors ? 'checked' : ''}>

      <span>Give Evening Prayer to Fr. Leckband and Fr. Lehman first

      <span class="sub">The one service they may liturgise. On, it lifts about 26 services a

      year off Fr. Leigeber and Fr. Suelzle each.</span></span></label>


    <div class="divider-l">Sunday rotation</div>

    <div class="fld"><label for="stAnchor">Celebrant this Sunday (${iso(W)})</label>

      <select id="stAnchor">

        <option value="LG" ${curSun === 'LG' ? 'selected' : ''}>Fr. Leigeber</option>

        <option value="SU" ${curSun === 'SU' ? 'selected' : ''}>Fr. Suelzle</option>

      </select>

      <div class="hint">Sets which man starts the alternation.</div></div>


    <div class="divider-l">Liturgical calendar</div>

    <div class="lit-coverage" id="litCoverage"></div>

    <div class="fld"><label for="litFile">Upload next year's calendar (.xlsx)</label>

      <input type="file" id="litFile" accept=".xlsx,.xlsm"></div>

    <div class="fld" id="litYearFld" style="display:none">

      <label for="litYear">Church year begins in</label>

      <input type="number" id="litYear" min="1900" max="2999" step="1">

      <div class="hint">The calendar year the file's first Sunday in Advent falls in.

        Change it and the preview below updates.</div>

    </div>

    <div id="litPreview"></div>

    <button class="btn2 gold" id="litImport" style="width:100%;display:none;margin-top:11px">

      Import this calendar</button>


    <div class="divider"></div>

    <button class="btn2 gold" id="stSave" style="width:100%">Save settings</button>

    <div class="hint" style="margin-top:9px">Choose <b>Fill schedule</b> afterwards to apply

      these to services that are not marked Kept.</div>

    <div class="divider"></div>

    <div class="divider-l">Danger</div>

    <button class="btn2 warn" id="stWipe" style="width:100%">Erase ${App.cursor.getFullYear()}</button>

    <div class="hint" style="margin-top:9px">Removes every service in that year for everyone.</div>

    <button class="btn2 warn" id="litReset" style="width:100%;margin-top:11px">

      Reset to the calendar built into this app</button>

    <div class="hint" style="margin-top:9px">Discards any uploaded calendar for everyone and

      returns to the one this roster started with.</div>`;


  el('scrim').classList.add('open');

  el('drawer').classList.add('open');


  /* ---- liturgical calendar coverage + import ---- */

  const litDates = Object.keys(App.liturgical).sort();

  el('litCoverage').innerHTML = litDates.length

    ? `<b>${litDates.length}</b> days loaded, <b>${fmtDateLong(litDates[0])}</b> –

       <b>${fmtDateLong(litDates[litDates.length - 1])}</b>.`

    : `No liturgical calendar loaded yet.`;


  let parsedGrid = null;   // cached raw grid from the last file read

  let parsedYear = null;

  let parsedCalendar = null;


  const litPreviewBox = (kind, html) => {

    el('litPreview').innerHTML = `<div class="lit-box ${kind}">${html}</div>`;

  };


  function refreshLitPreview() {

    if (!parsedGrid) return;

    const year = Math.round(Number(el('litYear').value)) || new Date().getFullYear();

    parsedYear = year;

    try {

      parsedCalendar = datesFromGrid(parsedGrid.rowsData, year);

      const dates = Object.keys(parsedCalendar).sort();

      if (!dates.length) {

        litPreviewBox('err', `<b>No days found.</b> This sheet doesn't look like a liturgical

          calendar in the expected layout.`);

        el('litImport').style.display = 'none';

        return;

      }

      const warn = dates.length < 300 || dates.length > 380;

      const fallbackNote = parsedGrid.usedFallback

        ? ` Used the sheet "${esc(parsedGrid.sheetName)}" — no sheet named "Calendar" was found.`

        : '';

      litPreviewBox(warn ? 'warn' : 'ok', `

        <b>${dates.length} days found</b>

        <span class="rng">${fmtDateLong(dates[0])} – ${fmtDateLong(dates[dates.length - 1])}</span>

        ${warn ? `<div style="margin-top:5px">That's ${dates.length < 300 ? 'fewer' : 'more'}

          than a full church year usually has. Check the year above is right.</div>` : ''}

        ${fallbackNote ? `<div style="margin-top:5px">${fallbackNote}</div>` : ''}

        <div style="margin-top:5px">Importing adds these dates to the calendar and replaces

        any that already exist for the same day.</div>`);

      el('litImport').style.display = 'block';

    } catch (e) {

      litPreviewBox('err', `<b>Couldn't read that as a calendar.</b> ${esc(e.message || '')}`);

      el('litImport').style.display = 'none';

    }

  }


  el('litFile').onchange = async () => {

    const file = el('litFile').files[0];

    parsedGrid = null; parsedCalendar = null;

    el('litYearFld').style.display = 'none';

    el('litImport').style.display = 'none';

    if (!file) { el('litPreview').innerHTML = ''; return; }

    litPreviewBox('busy', 'Reading the file…');

    try {

      const buf = await file.arrayBuffer();

      parsedGrid = await readWorkbookGrid(buf);

      const guess = (/(\d{4})/.exec(file.name) || [])[1];

      el('litYear').value = guess ? Number(guess) : new Date().getFullYear();

      el('litYearFld').style.display = 'block';

      refreshLitPreview();

    } catch (e) {

      litPreviewBox('err', `<b>Couldn't open that file.</b> ${esc(e.message ||

        'Make sure it\u2019s the .xlsx calendar file, not a copy saved in another format.')}`);

    }

  };

  el('litYear').addEventListener('input', () => { if (parsedGrid) refreshLitPreview(); });


  el('litImport').onclick = async () => {

    if (!parsedCalendar) return;

    const before = Object.keys(App.liturgical).length;

    App.liturgical = { ...App.liturgical, ...parsedCalendar };

    await saveLiturgical();

    const added = Object.keys(App.liturgical).length;

    render();

    const litDates2 = Object.keys(App.liturgical).sort();

    el('litCoverage').innerHTML = `<b>${litDates2.length}</b> days loaded, <b>${fmtDateLong(litDates2[0])}</b> –

       <b>${fmtDateLong(litDates2[litDates2.length - 1])}</b>.`;

    litPreviewBox('ok', `<b>Imported.</b> Everyone using this roster will see the new dates.`);

    el('litImport').style.display = 'none';

    toast(`Calendar imported — ${added - before >= 0 ? added - before : 0} new day${

      (added - before) === 1 ? '' : 's'} added, the rest updated.`);

  };


  el('litReset').onclick = () => {

    openDlg('Reset the liturgical calendar', `<p style="margin:0">This removes any imported

      calendar for everyone and returns to the one this roster started with. It cannot be undone.</p>`,

      `<button class="btn2" data-close>Keep it</button>

       <button class="btn2 warn" id="litResetGo">Reset</button>`);

    el('litResetGo').onclick = async () => {

      App.liturgical = { ...LITURGICAL_BUILTIN };

      await saveLiturgical();

      closeAll(); render();

      toast('Liturgical calendar reset to the built-in one.');

    };

  };


  el('stSave').onclick = async () => {

    const w = {};

    document.querySelectorAll('.stW').forEach(i => { w[i.dataset.p] = Number(i.value) || 0; });

    s.weights = w;

    s.parishName = el('stName').value.trim() || 'Pastoral Roster';

    s.leckbandInterval = Math.max(1, Number(el('stLk').value) || 6);

    s.maxSermonsPerWeek = Math.max(1, Number(el('stMax').value) || 2);

    s.capLeckbandToInterval = el('stCap').checked;

    s.eveningPrayerPrefersJuniors = el('stEp').checked;

    const want = el('stAnchor').value;

    s.alternationAnchor = ((((want === 'LG' ? 0 : 1) - wi) % 2) + 2) % 2;

    await saveSettings();

    closeAll(); render();

    toast('Settings saved. Choose Fill schedule to apply them.');

  };

  el('stWipe').onclick = () => {

    const y = App.cursor.getFullYear();

    openDlg('Erase ' + y, `<p style="margin:0">This removes every service in ${y} for everyone

      using this roster. It cannot be undone.</p>`,

      `<button class="btn2" data-close>Keep it</button>

       <button class="btn2 warn" id="wipeGo">Erase ${y}</button>`);

    el('wipeGo').onclick = () => {

      App.services = App.services.filter(x => !x.date.startsWith(String(y)));

      markDirty([y]); closeAll(); render(); toast(`${y} erased.`);

    };

  };

}


/* ---------- events ---------- */

document.addEventListener('click', e => {

  const t = e.target;

  if (t.closest('[data-close]') || t === el('scrim')) { closeAll(); return; }


  const tab = t.closest('#tabs button');

  if (tab) { App.view = tab.dataset.v; boot(); return; }


  if (t.closest('#prev') || t.closest('#next')) {

    const k = t.closest('#next') ? 1 : -1;

    const c = App.cursor;

    App.cursor = App.view === 'week' ? addDays(c, 7 * k)

      : App.view === 'month' ? new Date(c.getFullYear(), c.getMonth() + k, 1)

      : new Date(c.getFullYear() + k, c.getMonth(), 1);

    boot(); return;

  }

  if (t.closest('#today')) { App.cursor = new Date(); boot(); return; }

  if (t.closest('#refreshBtn')) {

    const before = App.services.length, beforeYears = { ...App.stamps };

    poll().then(() => {

      const same = Object.keys(beforeYears).every(y => beforeYears[y] === App.stamps[y]);

      if (same) toast('Up to date.');

    });

    return;

  }

  if (t.closest('#addSvc')) { openAdd(); return; }

  if (t.closest('#genBtn')) { openGenerate(); return; }

  if (t.closest('#exportBtn')) { openExport(); return; }

  if (t.closest('#setBtn')) { openSettings(); return; }


  const lock = t.closest('[data-lock]');

  if (lock) { toggleLock(lock.dataset.lock); return; }

  const del = t.closest('[data-del]');

  if (del) { removeService(del.dataset.del); return; }

  const add = t.closest('[data-add]');

  if (add) { openAdd(add.dataset.add); return; }


  if (t.closest('[data-restoreweek]')) {

    const days = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => iso(addDays(startOfWeek(App.cursor), i)));

    const back = removedOn(days);

    back.forEach(s => { s.deleted = false; });

    markDirty([...new Set(back.map(yearOf))]);

    render(); toast(`Put back ${back.length} service${back.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}.`);

    return;

  }

  const goto = t.closest('[data-goto]');

  if (goto) { App.cursor = fromISO(goto.dataset.goto); App.view = 'week'; boot(); return; }

  const mo = t.closest('[data-month]');

  if (mo) {

    App.cursor = new Date(App.cursor.getFullYear(), Number(mo.dataset.month), 1);

    App.view = 'month'; boot(); return;

  }

});


document.addEventListener('change', e => {

  const sel = e.target.closest('select[data-svc]');

  if (sel) onChangeAssign(sel.dataset.svc, sel.dataset.role, sel.value);

});


document.addEventListener('focusout', e => {

  const ta = e.target.closest('textarea.noteinput');

  if (!ta) return;

  const s = svc(ta.dataset.svc);

  if (!s) return;

  const val = ta.value.trim();

  if ((s.notes || '') === val) return;

  s.notes = val || null;

  s.locked = true;

  markDirty([yearOf(s)]);

  render();

});


document.addEventListener('input', e => {

  const ta = e.target.closest('textarea.noteinput');

  if (!ta) return;

  ta.style.height = 'auto';

  ta.style.height = ta.scrollHeight + 'px';

});


document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {

  if (e.key === 'Escape') closeAll();

});


/* ---------- password gate ----------

   Shown until the cached password (or one just entered) checks out against the

   database. The database is what actually enforces this — see kv_check_password

   in supabase-setup.sql — this UI is just the front door. */

function showGate(message) {

  el('scrim').classList.add('open');

  el('gate').classList.add('open');

  el('gateError').textContent = message || '';

  el('gatePw').value = '';

  setTimeout(() => el('gatePw').focus(), 50);

}

function hideGate() {

  el('gate').classList.remove('open');

  el('scrim').classList.remove('open');

}


async function attemptGate() {

  const pw = el('gatePw').value;

  if (!pw) { el('gateError').textContent = 'Enter the password.'; return; }

  el('gateSubmit').disabled = true;

  el('gateSubmit').textContent = 'Checking…';

  try {

    const ok = await checkPassword(pw);

    if (ok) {

      setCachedPassword(pw);

      authFailure = null;

      hideGate();

      await startApp();

    } else {

      el('gateError').textContent = 'That password is incorrect.';

    }

  } catch (e) {

    el('gateError').textContent = describeConnectionError(e);

  } finally {

    el('gateSubmit').disabled = false;

    el('gateSubmit').textContent = 'Enter';

  }

}


el('gateSubmit').addEventListener('click', attemptGate);

el('gatePw').addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') attemptGate(); });


/* ---------- boot ---------- */

async function boot() {

  await ensureYears(visibleYears());

  render();

}


async function startApp() {

  setSync('saving', 'loading…');

  const rec = await Store.get(SETTINGS_KEY);

  if (rec && rec.settings) App.settings = { ...App.settings, ...rec.settings };

  const litRec = await Store.get(LITURGICAL_KEY);

  if (litRec && litRec.calendar) {

    App.liturgical = litRec.calendar;

    App.liturgicalStamp = litRec.updatedAt || 0;

  }

  App.booted = true;

  await boot();

  setSync('idle', 'saved');

  setInterval(poll, 15000);

  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {

    if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') poll();

  });

}


(async function init() {

  if (!configured()) {

    App.booted = true;

    render();

    setSync('error', 'not connected');

    return;

  }

  sbClient = window.supabase.createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_ANON_KEY);


  const cached = getCachedPassword();

  if (cached) {

    setSync('saving', 'checking…');

    try {

      const ok = await checkPassword(cached);

      if (ok) { await startApp(); return; }

    } catch (e) { /* connection trouble — fall through to the gate either way */ }

  }

  App.booted = true;

  render();          // shows the empty shell behind the gate, not a blank page

  setSync('idle', '');

  showGate();

})();


</script>
